# Summer 2027 Software Engineering Internships

Read from employers' public job boards (Greenhouse, Workday, Lever, Ashby, SmartRecruiters and more) and refreshed every 30 minutes by [swejoblists.com](https://swejoblists.com/?type=internship&utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme&utm_campaign=internships) (free, no account). Last update: 2026-11-12 09:37 UTC.

This list shows only the roles labelled H-1B 🟢 Yes or 🟡 Likely. Every open role, whatever its label, is in [ALL_ROLES.md](ALL_ROLES.md).

Looking for new grad roles? See [TheoXiong7/2027-New-Grad-SWE-Jobs](https://github.com/TheoXiong7/2027-New-Grad-SWE-Jobs).

**Showing 932 of 3,517 open roles**: the ones labelled H-1B 🟢 Yes or 🟡 Likely, from **304 companies**, **158** added in the last 7 days. The other 2,585 (🟠 Unlikely, 🔴 No or not labelled yet) are in [ALL_ROLES.md](ALL_ROLES.md). Filters, company pages and match scores: [swejoblists.com/?type=internship](https://swejoblists.com/?type=internship&utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme&utm_campaign=internships).

**H-1B column**: 🟢 Yes / 🟡 Likely = how likely the employer sponsors an H-1B visa for the role. A model reads each posting; it is a screen, not a guarantee.
